- **distributed-transactions**: Atomic commits across shards, services, or regions when a single failure must not leave the system half-applied. Reach for this when you see "double-charged customer", "ghost orders", "inventory drift after partial outage", "stuck PREPARED transactions blocking the table", "coordinator crashed and locks held forever", "p99 spike after cross-region commit", or "we need read-your-writes across shards". Covers 2PC, 3PC, Percolator, Spanner/TrueTime, and the alternatives (Sagas, idempotent retries, outbox) you should usually pick instead. ([hung-phan/system-skills](https://github.com/hung-phan/system-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/system-review/references/data-systems/distributed-transactions))
